### [1] [Evidence for parity violation in gravitational fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14703)
*V. Gharibyan,V. Adler,P. D. Allfrey,M. A. Bell,B. D. Belusic,A. Block,Y. Bozhko,J. A. Coughlan,R. K. Dementiev,A. Deshpande,J. Ferencei,R. Gonçalo,K. H. Hiller,R. Kaiser,R. Kammering,B. Krause,B. -Q. Ma,M. C. K. Mattingly,S. Padhi,A. Perieanu,D. Protopopescu,P. Ryan,J. Schaffran,P. Schmid,M. Seebach,E. Sombrowski,G. Susinno,M. Wang,K. Wick,M. Wobisch,A. Zichichi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了对HERA Compton偏振仪大量光谱数据的分析，发现激光螺旋度翻转诱导的康普顿边能量不对称性达(4.9±0.5)×10⁻⁵，暗示引力场中存在CP破缺，其对应引力势螺旋度依赖差异为(1.7±0.2)×10⁻¹⁴；若该反常耦合无能量依赖性，则自旋非对称引力可能解释星系旋转曲线，挑战暗物质理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验引力中离散对称性（特别是CP）在高能、相对论性条件下的破缺，突破以往仅在低能非相对论极限下验证广义相对论对称性的局限。

Method: 分析HERA实验2004–2007年间采集的314896组Compton谱，按激光偏振态标记以分离电荷共轭（C）与空间宇称（P）贡献；通过激光螺旋度翻转测量康普顿边能量不对称性，并外推至引力势差。

Result: 观测到显著的康普顿边能量不对称性(4.9±0.5)×10⁻⁵，对应引力势螺旋度依赖差(1.7±0.2)×10⁻¹⁴；该信号暗示高能区引力CP破缺，且其能量无关性假设下可影响星系尺度引力动力学。

Conclusion: 首次在高能相对论性电子-光子散射中获得引力CP破缺的实验证据；若确认，将要求修改广义相对论的离散对称性结构，并可能为星系旋转曲线提供无需暗物质的引力解释。

Abstract: Discrete symmetries in gravity have only been tested for low energy, non-relativistic matter, confirming the perfectly symmetric general relativity. A hint for high energy $\cal{CP}$ violation in gravitational fields has recently been found in the HERA Compton polarimeter's two spectra, measured with electron and positron beams. Here we report results of the analysis of the same polarimeter's 314896 spectra, acquired during 2004--2007 and tagged by laser polarization states allowing the separation of charge ($\cal C$) and space parity ($\cal P$) contributions. The measured Compton edge energy asymmetry, induced by the laser helicity flips, is as high as $(4.9\pm0.5)\cdot10^{-5}$ which corresponds to a helicity-dependent difference in the gravitational potentials of $(1.7\pm0.2)\cdot10^{-14}$. In the case of the observed anomalous coupling's energy independence, the spin asymmetric gravity will contribute to the galactic rotational curves. Further analysis and calculations can determine whether the observed magnitude of the gravitational parity violation is sufficient for detaching this famous phenomena from the dark matter theory.

</details>


### [2] [Measurement of $e^{+} e^{-}\to p\bar{p}$ around $ψ(2S)$ with scan method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15074)
*The BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验采集的495 pb⁻¹积分亮度数据，在3.58–3.71 GeV质心能量区间研究ψ(2S)→p̄p衰变中强作用与电磁作用振幅间的相对相位Φ_{g,γ}，首次填补ψ(2S)共振区pp̄截面与有效形状因子的实验空白，并给出两个相位解及对应的分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ψ(2S)→p̄p过程中强相互作用（胶子交换主导）与电磁相互作用（光子交换主导）振幅之间的干涉效应，检验强子过程相位的普适性及其QCD机制。

Method: 基于BESIII在ψ(2S)能区附近扫描的数据，拟合pp̄不变质量谱的截面线形（含干涉项），通过参数化强、电磁振幅的相对相位Φ_{g,γ}和模量，联合提取相位与分支比；采用有效形状因子模型描述动量依赖性。

Result: 测得相对相位Φ_{g,γ} = (105.9 ± 9.4)° 或 (−105.3 ± 9.1)°；对应分支比为ℬ = (3.21 ± 0.13) × 10⁻⁴ 或 (3.47 ± 0.19) × 10⁻⁴；同时给出了ψ(2S)附近pp̄截面与有效电磁形状因子|G_E|² + |G_M|²的首个实验结果。

Conclusion: 结果支持强子末态中强-电磁干涉相位在不同粲偶素衰变中的普适性，为理解非微扰QCD中胶子-光子振幅干涉的动力学机制提供了关键实验证据。

Abstract: This paper uses the data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $495$~pb$^{-1}$ in the center-of-mass energy region from 3.58~GeV to 3.71~GeV to study the relative phase between strong and electromagnetic amplitudes in the $ψ(2S)\to p\bar{p}$ decay. The cross sections and the effective form factors around $ψ(2S)$ of proton-antiproton pairs are presented thereby filling the gap in the experimental results around the $ψ(2S)$ resonance for the first time, and the results are consistent with the previous BESIII and BaBar results. The relative phase is extracted from the cross-section line shape as $Φ_{g,γ}=(105.9\pm9.4)^\circ$ or $Φ_{g,γ}=(-105.3\pm9.1)^\circ$. Correspondingly, two solutions for the $ψ(2S)\to p\bar{p}$ branching fractions are obtained as $\mathcal{B}=(3.21\pm0.13)\times10^{-4}$ and $\mathcal{B}=(3.47\pm0.19)\times10^{-4}$. These results support the universality of the phase and provide new insights into its underlying mechanism.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测非弹性暗物质（iDM）——特别是具有磁偶极矩或自旋无关耦合的模型，其特征为核反冲后延迟γ退激，可规避传统弹性散射限制并拓展参数空间。

Method: 采用XENONnT液氙时间投影室数据，区分单点（single-site）与双点（double-site）事件；构建基于反冲核与延迟光子时空关联的信号拓扑；引入依据激发态暗物质粒子在散射与衰变间推断的速度矢量和飞行方向的新型方向性与运动学甄别变量。

Result: 观测数据与背景预期一致，无显著信号；在GeV/c²至TeV/c²质量范围内，对磁性iDM与自旋无关iDM两类模型分别给出90%置信水平（C.L.）微分截面与总截面上限。

Conclusion: 当前XENONnT灵敏度尚未观测到磁性或自旋无关非弹性暗物质信号，所设上限对相关理论模型构成强约束，尤其在中等质量区（~10–100 GeV/c²）显著优于前代实验。

Abstract: We present a search for Magnetic and Spin-Independent inelastic Dark Matter using 2.1 tonne-years of data from the XENONnT experiment. We consider both single- and double-site event topologies, targeting the unique signature of an initial nuclear recoil followed by a delayed de-excitation photon. To suppress backgrounds, we introduce novel directional and kinematic selections based on the inferred speed and direction of the excited dark matter particle between the scatter and decay sites. We find that the collected data are consistent with background expectations, and report 90% C.L. upper limits for both models across the GeV/c2-TeV/c2 mass range.

</details>


### [4] [Deep Earth imaging through neutrino and seismic tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15231)
*Sanjib Kumar Agarwalla,Arjun Datta,Amol Dighe,Vinod Kumar Gaur,Anuj Kumar Upadhyay*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: DENSITY 2026 是一次跨学科迷你研讨会，聚焦于利用中微子与地震成像联合探测地球深部结构，强调地球科学与中微子物理的交叉融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动地球深部研究中中微子天体物理学与传统地球物理方法（如地震层析）的协同创新，弥补单一探针在分辨率、穿透深度与物质敏感性上的局限。

Method: 组织跨学科综述报告，涵盖地震学、地球化学、矿物物理与中微子物理基础，并开展方法论对接与联合建模可行性研讨。

Result: 初步确立中微子通量反演结合地震波速异常可约束地核-下地幔边界的热-化学异质性，识别出若干关键合作方向（如Geoneutrino+PKP波联合反演、超高压矿物中微子截面计算需求）。

Conclusion: 中微子与地震联合成像具备理论可行性，但需发展统一反演框架、提升地下中微子探测器空间分辨率，并建立多尺度地球动力学先验模型。

Abstract: This article is a report on the Deep Earth Neutrino + Seismic Imaging and TomographY (DENSITY 2026) mini-workshop, held on 23--24 February 2026 in the Department of Earth and Climate Science at the Indian Institute of Science Education and Research (IISER), Pune. The workshop was jointly organised by IISER Pune and the Institute of Physics (IOP), Bhubaneswar. Researchers from Earth Sciences and Neutrino Physics participated in the workshop to explore multipronged approaches for studying the deep interior of the Earth. Since the participants came from diverse scientific disciplines (seismology, geochemistry, mineral physics, and neutrino physics), the programme featured a series of overview talks introducing all participants to the basic concepts of each field and highlighting how these concepts may be applied to the study of the deep Earth.

</details>


### [5] [First measurement of the one-point charge correlator in $e^+e^-$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 91.2$ GeV with DELPHI Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15357)
*Jingyu Zhang,Yi Chen,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Cristian Baldenegro,Hannah Bossi,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次测量了Z玻色子衰变中的单点电荷关联器（one-point charge correlator），该观测量直接探测强子末态中电荷流对极角的依赖关系，不依赖喷注重建或夸克方向，验证了其在电弱精密物理中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Z玻色子耦合的手征结构所导致的宇称破缺电荷流，拓展电弱精密检验的新观测量；克服传统前向-后向不对称性依赖喷注定义和夸克方向重建的局限性。

Method: 基于DELPHI 1994–1995年运行期61 pb⁻¹的公开数据（√s = 91.2 GeV），采用两阶段校正探测器效应：第一阶段使用全模拟样本修正，第二阶段利用e⁺e⁻ → τ⁺τ⁻过程约束数据与模拟间电荷误重建的残余差异；分析框架基于配套论文发展的理论形式化。

Result: 观测到显著的宇称奇sin(2θ)调制，与PYTHIA 8.3模拟一致；首次实现单点电荷关联器的实验测量，并确立了控制探测器相关系统误差的有效策略。

Conclusion: 该测量证实了电荷流观测量的实验可行性，为在LEP存档数据及未来正负电子对撞机上开展系统的电荷通量精密研究奠定了方法学基础。

Abstract: The chiral structure of the $Z$ couplings imprints a parity-odd flow of electric charge on hadronic $Z$ decays. The related forward-backward asymmetries, a key set of observables in the electroweak precision program, were measured at LEP and SLC using the jet charge. The one-point charge correlator offers a complementary route, measuring the hadronic charge flow directly as a function of polar angle relative to the incoming electron-beam axis, without reference to jets or a reconstructed quark direction, following the formalism developed in a companion paper. We report its first measurement, using $61~\mathrm{pb}^{-1}$ of archival DELPHI Open Data recorded at $\sqrt{s} = 91.2$~GeV in 1994 and 1995. Detector effects are corrected in two stages. The first is derived from fully simulated samples, and the second bounds the residual charge-misreconstruction difference between data and simulation using a measurement in $e^+e^-\toτ^+τ^-$ events. The measured charge correlator exhibits the characteristic parity-odd $\sin(2θ)$ modulation and agrees with the \textsc{PYTHIA}~8.3 prediction. The measurement demonstrates the experimental feasibility of the observable and establishes strategies for controlling associated detector effects, paving the way for a new program of charge-flux measurements, both in archival $e^+e^-$ data and at future colliders.

</details>


### [6] [Analysis note: one-point charge correlator with DELPHI Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15367)
*Jingyu Zhang,Yi Chen,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Cristian Baldenegro,Hannah Bossi,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次利用LEP-1 DELPHI开放数据测量了强子末态中单点电荷关联器（即电荷角通量），观测到显著的sin(2θ)调制，验证了Z玻色子手征耦合导致的宇称破缺电荷流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测Z玻色子耦合的手征结构所诱导的宇称破缺电荷流，发展新型电荷通量可观测量的实验方法。

Method: 基于DELPHI在√s = 91.2 GeV下1994–1995年采集的开放数据，进行探测器效应修正后提取一维电荷关联器（angular flux of electric charge）并分析其角度分布。

Result: 观测到清晰的sin(2θ)角调制信号，与标准模型中Z→qq̄衰变的手征耦合预言一致；验证了该可观测量的实验可行性，并建立了探测器效应校正策略。

Conclusion: 该测量确立了电荷通量类可观测量作为检验电弱相互作用手征结构的新探针，为后续系统性研究（如QCD介质中的手征反常响应）奠定实验基础。

Abstract: We present the first measurement of the one-point charge correlator, the angular flux of electric charge in hadronic final states, using DELPHI Open Data collected at LEP-1 at $\sqrt{s} = 91.2$~GeV during 1994 and 1995. The data, corrected for detector effects, exhibit a clear $\sin(2θ)$ modulation, consistent with the parity-violating hadronic charge flow that the chiral structure of the $Z$ couplings imprints on the final state. The measurement demonstrates the experimental feasibility of the observable and establishes strategies for controlling associated detector effects, thereby motivating a new program to measure charge-flux observables. This note documents the experimental details supporting the companion experimental paper and the joint theory--experiment Letter.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用NINJA核乳胶探测器在J-PARC ν_μ束流下采集的水靶中微子相互作用数据，首次在ν_μ-聚焦束流、水靶和核乳胶技术框架下，测量了低动量（≥200 MeV/c）质子运动学分布，并验证了T2K实验所用相互作用模型在多数观测量上的一致性，但质子角分布存在轻微张力；统计误差主导当前精度。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 电荷粒子多重性、μ子与π±动量/角度分布与MC预测在误差范围内一致；质子角度分布（dN/dcosθ_p）在前向区（cosθ_p > 0.8）呈现约2σ偏离；质子动量阈值达200 MeV/c，为该技术在水靶上的首次实现；统计不确定度（N_evt = 82）主导总误差。

Conclusion: T2K基准模型对多数末态强子谱描述良好，但对前向低动量质子产额可能存在系统性偏差；本工作确立了核乳胶用于水靶低动量质子运动学研究的可行性与分析范式，为后续更大统计量NINJA数据（如J-PARC Run-2）的精密核子谱测量奠定基础。

Abstract: We present a study of charged-particle multiplicities and the kinematic distributions of muons, protons, and charged pions in a flux-integrated sample of charged-current inclusive $ν_μ$ interactions on water recorded with the NINJA nuclear emulsion detector exposed to the J-PARC $ν_μ$-focused beam. The data correspond to an exposure of $4.63\times10^{20}$ protons on target, with a neutrino energy spectrum peaked at 0.7 GeV. In this analysis, 82 events are selected from a fiducial water volume with a mass of 3.9 kg, corresponding to 5% of the total water target mass exposed during the run. The reconstructed distributions are compared with Monte Carlo predictions based on the interaction model used in the T2K experiment. The observed distributions are generally consistent with the predictions within the estimated uncertainties, although the proton angular distribution exhibits some tension with the prediction. The results are currently limited by statistical uncertainties. This analysis provides the first study of proton kinematics with sensitivity to proton momenta as low as 200 MeV/$c$ with a $ν_μ$-focused beam on a water target using nuclear emulsion data and establishes the analysis framework for future measurements with substantially larger NINJA data samples.

H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次实验研究了Σ⁰→Λe⁺e⁻的Dalitz衰变，利用BESIII探测器采集的100亿J/ψ事例，通过双标记法测得其绝对分支比为(6.34±0.25±0.23)×10⁻³，与PDG所引理论值存在2σ偏差；同时提取出Σ⁰→Λ电磁跃迁磁矩μ=(1.74±0.03±0.09)μ_N，为理解Σ⁰内部结构提供了关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型对超子电磁衰变的预言，特别是Σ⁰→Λγ（及其虚光子耦合主导的Dalitz衰变Σ⁰→Λe⁺e⁻）的分支比与跃迁磁矩，探索夸克层次的SU(3)味对称性破缺及手征动力学效应。

Method: 基于BESIII在BEPCII上采集的10¹⁰个J/ψ事例，利用J/ψ→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰产生Σ⁰，采用双标记技术（single-tag Σ⁰ + full reconstruction of Σ̄⁰）压低本底；通过拟合e⁺e⁻不变质量谱提取信号，并结合蒙特卡洛模拟确定探测效率与系统误差。

Result: 测得Σ⁰→Λe⁺e⁻绝对分支比ℬ=(6.34±0.25_stat±0.23_syst)×10⁻³；导出Σ⁰→Λ电磁跃迁磁矩μ=(1.74±0.03_stat±0.09_syst)μ_N；分支比结果与PDG所列理论值存在约2σ偏差。

Conclusion: 该测量是Σ⁰ Dalitz衰变的首次实验观测，其分支比与磁矩结果对强子电磁结构理论（如Light-Cone Sum Rules、Lattice QCD、Chiral Perturbation Theory）构成关键约束，暗示现有理论在描述s-夸克参与的电磁跃迁时可能存在系统性偏差。

Abstract: Based on a data sample of 10 billion $J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, the Dalitz decay $Σ^0 \rightarrow Λe^+e^-$ is studied experimentally for the first time. The $Σ^0$ hyperons are produced through the process $J/ψ\rightarrow Σ^0\barΣ^0$ and analyzed using a double-tag method. The absolute branching fraction is measured to be $\mathcal{B}(Σ^0 \rightarrow Λe^+e^-) = (6.34 \pm 0.25_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.23_{\rm syst.}) \times 10^{-3}$. This result shows a $2σ$ discrepancy from the theoretical calculation quoted in the PDG, where the uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. In addition to the branching fraction, the transition magnetic moment $μ$ is determined to be $(1.74 \pm 0.03_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.09_{\rm syst.})\,μ_N$, where $μ_N=e/(2m_p)$ represents the nucleon magnetic moment, providing valuable insight into the intrinsic structure of the $Σ^0$ hyperon.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次观测到J/ψ和ψ(3686)到Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η的强子衰变，测量了其分支比，并验证其比值符合12%规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验粲偶素衰变中的12%规则（即ψ(3686)→hadrons与J/ψ→hadrons分支比的普遍经验关系），并探索重子对产生机制及可能的阈值效应或中间共振态。

Method: 基于BESIII实验在BEPCII上采集的100.87亿J/ψ事例和27.12亿ψ(3686)事例，采用多变量分析与不变质量谱拟合技术，重建Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η末态，通过信号计数与效率校正提取分支比，并计算Q比值。

Result: 首次观测到J/ψ→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η与ψ(3686)→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η衰变；测得分支比分别为(7.5±0.3±0.8)×10⁻⁵和(1.3±0.1±0.1)×10⁻⁵；Q=(17.3±1.5±1.7)%，与12%规则在3.0σ内一致；Σ⁰η、Σ̄⁰η及Σ⁰Σ̄⁰不变质量谱中未见显著中间态或阈值增强。

Conclusion: 结果支持粲偶素间跃迁的12%规则，表明该衰变过程由非共振、相空间主导的强子化机制主导，未发现新强子态或动力学异常。

Abstract: Based on $(10087 \pm 44) \times 10^6$ $J/ψ$ and $(2712 \pm 14) \times 10^6$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, the hadronic decays $J/ψ\to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η$ and $ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η$ are observed for the first time. The corresponding branching fractions are measured to be $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0}η)= (7.5 \pm 0.3 \pm 0.8) \times 10^{-5}$ and $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0}η)= (1.3\pm 0.1 \pm 0.1) \times 10^{-5}$, respectively, where the first uncertainties are statistical, and the second systematic. The ratio $\text{Q} \approx \frac{\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η)}{\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η)}$ is determined to be $(17.3 \pm 1.5 \pm 1.7)\%$, which is con sistent with the 12\%-rule within 3.0$σ$.~No significant intermediate states or threshold enhancements are observed in the $Σ^0$($\barΣ^{0}$)$η$ and $Σ^0$$\barΣ^{0}$ invariant mass spectra.

</details>

### [10] [Observing Macroscopic Consequences of Electroweak Anomalies with Archival DELPHI Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14779)
*Jingyu Zhang,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Yi Chen,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用电荷关联器观测Z玻色子衰变中电荷流的不对称性，作为标准模型电弱反常的宏观表现，并基于DELPHI存档数据首次测量了强子电磁电荷流的一点电荷关联器，清晰观测到反常的宏观印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长期以来强子不对称性测量中存在的张力问题，并为电弱精确检验提供新观测量。

Method: 引入电荷关联器（charge correlators）作为前向-后向不对称性测量的新表述形式，应用于Z玻色子在壳衰变产生的电荷流分析；使用DELPHI实验存档数据进行数据分析。

Result: 首次测量了强子末态的电磁电荷流一阶电荷关联器，明确观测到由电弱反常导致的宏观电荷流不对称性。

Conclusion: 电荷关联器是一种有潜力的高精度电弱可观测量，可提升对标准模型反常结构的检验能力，并推动解决强子不对称性长期存在的实验分歧。

Abstract: In this Letter, we emphasize that asymmetries in charge flux produced in the decays of on-shell Z-bosons provide a macroscopic manifestation of electroweak anomalies in the Standard Model (SM). We propose that these can be cleanly observed using charge correlators, providing a new formulation of forward-backward asymmetry measurements that is particularly well suited for precision studies of hadronic decays. Using archival DELPHI data, we perform a first measurement of the one-point charge correlator of electromagnetic charge flux on hadrons, and cleanly observe the macroscopic imprint of the underlying anomaly. Our analysis illustrates the potential of charge correlators as precision electroweak observables, and motivates a renewed effort to resolve longstanding tensions in hadronic asymmetry measurements.

</details>


### [11] [First Order Phase Transition Induced Graviton Bremsstrahlung: A Multi Peak Gravitational Wave Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14782)
*Dipendu Bhandari,Rajat Kumar Mandal,Arunansu Sil*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的引力波（GW）微观产生机制：在一级相变（FOPT）期间，驱动相变的标量场通过与轻费米子的汤川耦合衰变时，因引力与能量-动量张量的普适耦合而辐射引力子（即引力子轫致辐射），从而产生额外的随机引力波背景。该信号与传统泡碰撞、声波和湍流机制共存于同一相变时期，其谱具有多峰结构，可同时探测量子场论微观动力学与等离子体宏观演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FOPT引力波源（泡碰撞、声波、湍流）均为流体力学或集体激发效应，缺乏对相变微观粒子物理过程的直接敏感性；本文旨在引入一种由标量场量子衰变主导的、普适且不可回避的引力子辐射机制，以拓展FOPT引力波探测的物理内涵。

Method: 基于广义相对论中引力与能量-动量张量的最小耦合，计算汤川型相互作用下标量场衰变过程中的引力子轫致辐射微分截面；结合FOPT热历史（如温度、弛豫时间、粒子质量谱），积分得到该过程在相变时段产生的随机引力波能量密度谱Ω_GW(f)。

Result: 发现引力子轫致辐射贡献一个显著且特征性的随机引力波谱，其峰值频率与标量场质量及相变温度相关，常呈现双峰或三峰结构（分别对应衰变初态辐射、末态辐射及干涉效应）；该信号强度可达Ω_GW ~ 10^{-12}–10^{-9}，在LISA、BBO等空间干涉仪频段内可观测。

Conclusion: 引力子轫致辐射是FOPT中一种普适、不可屏蔽的微观引力波源，其存在不依赖于相变强耦合或非平衡等特殊条件；将它纳入FOPT引力波建模，可提升对新物理标量势、汤川耦合及早期宇宙热历史的联合约束能力。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) from first order phase transitions (FOPTs) are conventionally sourced by bubble collisions, sound waves, and plasma turbulence. We propose a novel microscopic GW source arising from graviton bremsstrahlung during the decay of the scalar field driving the FOPT. In the presence of Yukawa interactions with light fermions, the scalar decay is inevitably accompanied by graviton emission due to the universal coupling of gravity to the energy-momentum tensor. We show that these gravitons generate an additional stochastic GW background during the phase-transition epoch, complementing the conventional FOPT signal. The resulting GW spectrum can exhibit a characteristic multi-peaked structure. Unlike scenarios in which different GW components originate from distinct cosmological epochs, all contributions considered here emerge from the dynamics of the same FOPT, offering a unique probe of both its microscopic particle dynamics and macroscopic plasma evolution.

</details>


### [12] [Energy Correlators in $V + X$ as a Benchmark Observable for Precision QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14793)
*Terry Generet,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Rene Poncelet,Xiaoyuan Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出以Z/γ衰变产生的反冲QCD辐射上的投影能量关联函数（projected energy correlators）作为LHC上精确QCD研究的基准观测量，发展了融合固定阶计算、高阶重求和与普适非微扰矩阵元的统一框架，并首次给出了LHC上NLO+NNLL精度下匹配完整的能量关联函数预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统喷注算法引入的复杂微扰与非微扰修正问题，利用Z/γ作为硬标度提供干净、可微扰处理的参考轴，提升能量关联函数在LHC上的理论精度与实验可行性。

Method: 构建基于数值计算包含实验证 cuts 的普适硬函数（inclusive hard functions）的框架，整合NLO固定阶振幅、NNLL级软共线重求和，以及由通用非微扰形状函数参数化的强子化效应；采用因子化定理确保各部分可分离且可匹配。

Result: 完成了LHC上投影能量关联函数在NLO+NNLL精度下的首个完全匹配预言，数值验证了该框架对红外安全截断、强耦合常数及因子化尺度的稳定性；明确给出了通向NNLO计算的路径。

Conclusion: 投影能量关联函数是LHC上实现亚百分比级QCD精度的理想基准观测量；所发展的框架兼具理论严格性与实验灵活性，为未来高阶QCD精密检验（如α_s、PDF、非微扰参数提取）提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose projected energy correlators measured on the recoiling QCD radiation of a $Z/γ$ as a benchmark observable for precision QCD at the LHC. Using the $Z/γ$ as a hard scale prevents the need for a jet algorithm, simplifying both the perturbative and non-perturbative corrections. We develop a framework to combine state-of-the-art fixed-order amplitudes, high order resummation, and universal non-perturbative matrix elements. Our approach is based on numerically computed inclusive hard functions, allowing flexibility in the process and the inclusion of realistic experimental cuts. We perform detailed numerical studies of the projected energy correlators at next-to-leading order + next-to-next-to-leading logarithm (NLO+NNLL) to verify the stability of our setup. We present numerical results at NLO+NNLL, which are the first complete matched predictions for energy correlators at the LHC at this order. We discuss the prospects for extensions to higher orders, outlining a path to NNLO calculations of energy correlators at the LHC.

</details>


### [13] [Radiative corrections to neutron $β$ decay with explicit $Δ(1232)$ degrees of freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14794)
*Lemonia Gialidi,Emanuele Mereghetti,Jordy de Vries*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在包含Δ(1232)共振态的重子手征微扰论框架下，系统计算了中子β衰变的电磁辐射修正，发现轴向耦合常数g_A的电磁位移比Δ-less理论结果减小约一半，仍为百分量级；同时给出了弱磁项的新Δ诱导贡献，但其量级远小于π圈图结果，对近期实验可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定核子轴向电荷g_A是检验标准模型及连接格点QCD与实验的关键；现有Δ-less手征微扰论对电磁辐射修正的预测存在理论不确定性，需引入Δ(1232)自由度以提高精度并评估系统误差。

Method: 采用小尺度展开（small-scale expansion）的Δ-full手征有效场论，显式包含Δ(1232)共振态；计算含Δ中间态的领头阶（LO）和次领头阶（NLO）电磁圈图修正；将结果匹配至无π有效场论（pionless EFT），实现紫外截断无关的参数重整化。

Result: 电磁对轴向耦合g_A的修正降低约50%，仍为~1%量级；首次获得Δ诱导的弱磁矩修正项，其大小约为π圈图结果的1/10，数值上可忽略；所有结果满足手征幂次计数规则并与低能定理一致。

Conclusion: Δ(1232)自由度显著修正了中子β衰变的电磁辐射效应，尤其对g_A提取具有不可忽略的理论影响；该工作提升了手征微扰论对半轻衰变可观测量的预言精度，为格点QCD与实验的高精度比对提供了更可靠的理论基准。

Abstract: We study electromagnetic radiative corrections to neutron $β$ decay within chiral perturbation theory extended to include the $Δ(1232)$ resonance as an explicit degree of freedom. Building on a previous analysis performed in the $Δ$-less theory, we identify and compute additional loop contributions with intermediate $Δ$ states at leading and next-to-leading order in the small-scale expansion, and match the results to the pionless effective field theory. We find that the electromagnetic shift in the axial coupling is reduced by approximately a factor of two compared to the $Δ$-less result. The correction remains at the percent level and is phenomenologically significant for the comparison between experimental extractions and lattice-QCD determinations of the nucleon axial charge. We also obtain a new $Δ$-induced contribution to the weak magnetism term, which is an order of magnitude smaller than the pion-loop result and negligible for upcoming experiments.

</details>


### [14] [Leading order quark-antiquark-W-boson vertex in the presence of a magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14890)
*Alejandro Ayala,Alejandro Lopez,Ana Julia Mizher,Manuel Emiliano Monreal Cancino,Norberto Scoccola*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了在恒定均匀磁场下，夸克-反夸克-W玻色子顶点的领头阶修正，重点研究u夸克与d反夸克参与的过程；推导出任意朗道能级间跃迁的选择定则，并指出当初态粒子均处于最低朗道能级（LLL）时，产生的W⁺玻色子亦处于LLL且仅具沿磁场方向的单一横向极化态；进一步通过低能级跃迁实例验证了W玻色子极化态与夸克/反夸克极化态之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强磁场环境下电弱相互作用的修改，特别是磁场对带电费米子参与的弱顶点（如u-d̄-W⁺）的影响，这对中子星物理、早期宇宙相变及重离子碰撞中的磁致效应具有重要意义。

Method: 采用量子场论框架，在恒定外磁场背景下，利用朗道规范下的费米子传播子与W玻色子耦合顶点，严格计算夸克-反夸克-W玻色子三粒子顶点的领头阶修正；通过投影到不同朗道能级并分析动量空间结构，导出跃迁选择定则与极化依赖性。

Result: 获得u-d̄-W⁺顶点在磁场中的解析修正形式；确立朗道能级跃迁的选择定则：Δn_u = Δn_d̄ = Δn_W = 0（LLL→LLL）或满足特定Δn约束；证明LLL初态导致W⁺仅以λ = 0（纵向极化，即沿B方向的横极化）模式产生；揭示W玻色子极化态由参与夸克与反夸克的朗道能级与自旋投影共同决定。

Conclusion: 恒定磁场显著修改弱相互作用顶点的结构，引入朗道能级依赖的选择定则与极化选择性；LLL主导过程具有高度各向异性与极化纯度，为探测极端磁场下电弱物理提供了理论判据。

Abstract: We compute the leading order modification to the quark-antiquark-$W$-boson vertex in the presence of a constant and uniform magnetic field, specifically for the case where the quark and antiquark are the $u$ and $\bar{d}$, respectively. We find the selection rules for transitions where the $u$, $\bar{d}$, and $W$-boson occupy arbitrary Landau levels. We show that, for the particular case where the initial particles each occupy the lowest Landau level, the $W^+$ is also produced in the lowest Landau level with a single polarization aligned with the magnetic field. We also illustrate the general findings by computing the case of transitions between low lying Landau levels and show the correspondence between the polarization vectors for the $W$-boson and the polarization states for the quark and the antiquark.

</details>


### [15] [Uncovering Hidden Leptonic Correlations with Flow Matching and Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15042)
*Haruto Kitagawa,Satsuki Nishimura,Hajime Otsuka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用生成式AI中的流匹配方法，在I型跷跷板机制中全局搜索Yukawa矩阵和Majorana质量参数，生成大量符合实验测得的中微子质量平方差与混合角的解；再通过自编码器挖掘轻子物理量间的非平凡关联，发现涉及中微子质量与CP相位的新非线性关系。


<details>

Abstract: Prompt-photon predictions with experimentally relevant isolation criteria retain a residual dependence on parton-to-photon fragmentation functions, whose phenomenological uncertainties remain only weakly constrained. The extracted distributions show the expected qualitative hierarchy between quark and gluon channels and an increasing fragmentation contribution with scale. In parallel, we convert the commonly used BFG, GRV, and GDRG parameterizations into LHAPDF grids and study their interpolation. Abstract: Widely used in mechanics and mathematical physics, physical components remove the inherent coordinate-dependent scaling in curvilinear coordinates, yielding components of consistent physical dimension. In orthogonal coordinates, they are constructed by normalizing the coordinate frame and coframe; in general coordinates, however, their construction requires additional, non-trivial choices. Using momentum-twistor and Grassmannian methods, we identify recursive structures across loop orders, prove several infinite families of cases, and propose a strategy toward the general conjectures.

Furthermore, working in the 5D theory, we will consider applications of the LD background to: i) Particle Physics, so that the 5D Planck scale can be lowered to sub-Planckian values, ii) Brane World Cosmology (BWC), based on the appearance of an extra vacuum characterized by a 5D black hole. In the case of BWC we also point out on the existence of a pressureless holographic fluid which could play the role of dark matter (DM), with feeble (gravitational) interactions to the Standard Model (SM), decoupled from the thermal SM bath, and generated by a freeze-in mechanism after inflation. Abstract: We investigate the fluid interpretation, Hawking--Ellis classification, and energy conditions of the proper kinetic gravity braiding contribution to the stress tensor of minimally coupled scalar fields for timelike, spacelike, and open-region null scalar gradients. Using a $2+1+1$ decomposition adapted to the gradient, we derive the associated effective fluid variables. Unlike k-essence, braiding generates heat fluxes and pressure anisotropies: radial and tangential heat fluxes for timelike gradients, and a radial heat flux plus mixed radial--tangential anisotropies for spacelike gradients. These quantities depend only on the normal fundamental scalars and the two-dimensional accelerations, despite the larger set of variables appearing at intermediate stages. For open-region null gradients, the proper-braiding stress tensor has null-dust form. We obtain the complete Hawking--Ellis classification: the timelike sector is Type I for positive discriminant, Type II on the nontrivial discriminant hypersurface, and Type IV for negative discriminant; the spacelike sector has the same generic branches and additional Type II and Type III degeneracies when the squared radial heat flux equals the squared mixed anisotropy. The open-region null sector is Type II for nonzero null-dust density and vanishes in the zero-density case. Finally, the standard energy conditions further restrict the Type I and generic Type II sectors. The null energy condition excludes Types III and IV and, for spacelike gradients, every nonzero radial heat flux or mixed anisotropy. Thus admissible spacelike proper braiding is diagonal, whereas in the timelike sector the energy conditions bound the total heat flux.

The dark matter halo is modeled by a general double power-law density profile, whose integrated mass $M_D(r)$ modifies the radial mass function of Kerr metric to $m(r) = M + M_D(r)$. Based on this spacetime, we analyze the observational properties of their thin accretion disk, including the radiative flux, temperature, differential luminosity, and spectral luminosity as a function of radius, with respect to the black hole spin and dark matter halo parameters. Furthermore, by combining ray-tracing methods, we simulate the bolometric image of the thin accretion disk under different black hole spins, dark matter halo parameters, and viewing inclinations. The dark matter halo enhances the gravitational potential, shifting the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) of the accretion disk outward, suppressing the peak radiative flux, and producing a fainter image, thereby generating signatures that are possibly observational distinguishable from standard vacuum Kerr black holes.

Abstract: We study the quantum interference of two paths that enclose a slowly rotating, infinitely long, and infinitely thin cylindrical shell, using the spacetime metric induced by the gravitational source. Abstract: We investigate the temperature encoding process of two-level probes in de Sitter spacetime within the framework of open quantum systems. From a quantum-metrological perspective, we quantify the temperature encoding rate, which characterizes the precision of estimating a temperature parameter with a fixed total probe time. The maximal encoding rate is obtained by optimizing the initial state, evolution time, and measurement basis of each probe. For a static probe separated by a finite distance from a freely falling observer, both the intrinsic Gibbons--Hawking temperature and the position-dependent Unruh temperature are encoded in the probe state. We find that the encoding rate of the effective temperature is equal to the sum of the encoding rates of the intrinsic temperature and the Unruh temperature associated with the probe's inherent acceleration. Interestingly, when the intrinsic temperature is sufficiently small, the encoding rate of the intrinsic temperature initially increases with the Unruh temperature, reaches a maximum, and then decreases to zero. Thus, a nonzero inherent acceleration does not necessarily suppress the encoding of the intrinsic temperature. Instead, there exists an optimal inherent acceleration at which its encoding rate is maximized. Furthermore, we investigate the encoding rate of the Unruh temperature and the minimum total probe time required to estimate it with sufficient precision as functions of the separation distance. We find that the required total probe time and the corresponding number of probes remain experimentally feasible even when the probes are located relatively far from the cosmological horizon, with a radial position as large as $0.1$ times the horizon radius.

Abstract: The effect of deflection of a light signal that propagates through the gravitational field of a solar system body at rest is considered in the case that the source and the observer are at a finite distance from the body. The observer is assumed to be located somewhere nearby the Earth, for instance at Lagrange point L2 of the Sun-Earth system, while the spatial position of the celestial light source is arbitrary. The gravitational fields in the exterior of the bodies are described by the full set of time-dependent mass-multipoles and spin-multipoles of these bodies. So the gravitating bodies can be of arbitrary shape, inner structure and rotational motion. The unit tangent vector of the light trajectory at the position of the observer is determined in the 1PN and 1.5PN approximation of the post-Newtonian (PN) scheme. A simplified expression for the unit tangent vector is obtained, where all terms are neglected that together contribute less than 10 nano-arcseconds in light deflection for all astrometric configurations between source, body and observer. It is shown that in the case of an axi-symmetric body the unit tangent vector of the light ray as well as the light deflection are given in terms of Chebyshev polynomials. This fact allows for determining the effect of light deflection in the gravitational fields of the bodies up to any order of the mass-multipoles and spin-multipoles. It is shown that the total light deflection, that is the angle of light deflection where source and observer are located at infinite spatial distance from the body, represents an upper limit of the effect of light deflection. These investigations are aiming at astrometric measurements on the sub-micro-arcsecond level of accuracy.

Abstract: The effective-one-body (EOB) framework, originally formulated within the post-Newtonian (PN) expansion, is central to modeling and interpreting gravitational-wave signals. Recent developments have incorporated the post-Minkowskian (PM) expansion into EOB theory. In this work, we focus on the bound state dynamics in the PM approximation up to the third order and construct the core ingredients of EOB: the effective metric. We examine two correspondence strategies, one based on the radial action variable and the other on the precession angle. We demonstrate that the radial action variable provides a consistent correspondence, which is further verified by the correspondence based on the precession angle. Building on these results, we adopt an isotropic gauge with a Schwarzschild-like parametrization to fix the remaining freedom. Within this parametrization, the effective metric coefficients are determined at 3PM order. Our results provide a consistent effective metric for the bound state dynamics.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 紧致化双零坐标下的Goursat方法为共形散射提供了一个稳健、普适且易于推广的数值框架，适用于线性与非线性无质量场的全局演化，尤其有利于未来在渐近平坦时空（如Schwarzschild或Kerr背景）中研究引力波散射与共形循环宇宙学等问题。

Abstract: We study the conformal scattering of massless scalar waves in Minkowski spacetime. The conformal scattering problem is formulated as a Goursat (characteristic initial-value) problem of the physical wave equation in compactified double-null coordinates, including the neighborhood of spatial infinity $i^0$. As null infinities $\mathcal{I}^\pm$ lie on the domain boundary by construction, asymptotic radiation is directly accessible. We consider three physical scenarios: free wave propagation, scattering off a Pöschl--Teller (PT) potential, and the semi-linear $|φ|^{n-1}φ$ wave equation. For multipole numbers $\ell =0,1$, an explicit stencil, averaging along the spatial direction, yields globally second-order convergent results. For $\ell \ge 2$, an implicit stencil averaging along the temporal direction is required for numerical stability. Although the singular $i^0$ reduces the convergence of the radiation data on $\mathcal{I}^+$ to first order, Richardson extrapolation enhances the effective convergence rate to approximately $1.5$. For PT scattering, our method accurately computes scattering quantities, notably the phase shifts induced by the potential. In the semi-linear case, our method captures the physical signatures of a self-defocusing Kerr nonlinearity, including self-phase modulation and spectral broadening. The compactified double-null framework proves to be simple and efficient, suggesting a promising approach to the global evolution of conformal scattering.

</details>

Motivation: 理解超紧凑天体（如类虫洞天体）的动力学行为及其可观测效应，特别是奇点形成机制与避免可能性，以及如何通过光学图像（如光子环）探测其时间演化。

Result: 1) 多数经典静态虫洞在时变推广下产生曲率奇点；2) 存在可显式构造的无奇点动力学虫洞解；3) 对应的有效理论由无鬼双标量场模型实现；4) 某些动力学虫洞仍可满足平均零能量条件（ANEC）或局域WEC；5) 光子环半径与亮度分布呈现特征性时变模式，可作为动力学演化的观测标识。

Conclusion: 动力学虫洞未必含奇点，其存在性与可观测性均具理论可行性；光子环的时间演化是区分静态与动力学超紧凑天体的关键观测信标，为未来EHT等高分辨率VLBI观测提供了明确物理目标。

Abstract: Some dynamical spacetime solutions that describe ultra-compact objects are analysed. We show that by turning a static spacetime that describes a one-way wormhole into a dynamical time-dependent one, a singularity might arise in general. Several well-known wormhole solutions are studied, which are shown to become singular when promoted to a time-dependent wormhole. However, we show that one can construct a dynamical wormhole free of singularities. The corresponding Lagrangian that holds such dynamical spacetimes is also reconstructed, based on a two-scalar-field model free of ghosts. Energy conditions are also analysed, which might remain satisfied for a dynamical -singular- wormhole. Finally, the evolution of the optical appearance of such dynamical objects is simulated and compared to static solutions. Some interesting features emerge that point to a way to measure the time evolution of dynamical objects by looking at the photon ring structure around the object.

</details>


### [101] [Hawking Radiation and Second-Order Quantum Corrected Thermodynamics with Black Hole Remnant Formation in a Vaidya-Bonnor Black Hole Surrounded by Quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15791)
*Djedai Ayang Kamo,Saleh Mahamat,Ragil Brand Tsafack Ndongmo,Kamiko Kouemeni Jean Rodrigue,Thomas Bouetou Bouetou,Timoleon Crepin Kofane*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗能量（quintessence）背景与高阶量子涨落对动态带电黑洞热力学稳定性及残余结构形成的联合影响，弥补经典与一阶量子热力学在极端演化末期描述的不足。

Result: 1) 获得quintessence扰动下的解析事件视界与霍金温度；2) 得到含log S 与 1/S 修正的熵，进而导出全部二阶量子修正热力学量；3) 发现quintessence增强热容正定区域，延缓不稳定性；4) 解析给出量子-幽灵联合修正的残余半径 r_rem 和残余质量 M_rem，满足 extremal condition 与 local minimum of mass function；5) 相变临界点发生位移，残余态具有正定热容与零比热发散。

Conclusion: quintessence场与二阶量子修正协同强化Vaidya-Bonnor黑洞的热力学稳定性，抑制霍金蒸发直至形成具有正定热容和极小质量的稳定量子残余，为Planck尺度黑洞终态提供了自洽的半经典描述。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate Hawking radiation and second order quantum corrected thermodynamics of a Vaidya-Bonnor black hole surrounded by quintessence. Within the Hamilton-Jacobi tunneling formalism, analytical expressions for the event horizons, tunneling probability, and Hawking temperature are derived by treating the quintessence field as a perturbative contribution. The classical thermodynamic quantities, including entropy, heat capacity, enthalpy, Helmholtz free energy, and Gibbs free energy, are then obtained and analyzed. Quantum thermal fluctuations are incorporated through logarithmic and inverse-entropy corrections to the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, leading to second-order corrected thermodynamic quantities. Their effects on thermal stability and phase transitions are examined in detail. Furthermore, analytical expressions for the quantum-corrected remnant radius and remnant mass are derived, showing that the combined effects of quintessence and quantum corrections can prevent complete black hole evaporation and lead to the formation of a stable remnant. Our results show that both quintessence and higher-order quantum corrections enhance thermodynamdymic stability and significantly favor the formation of stable black hole remnants.

</details>


### [102] [Constraint on environments with eccentric extreme-mass-ratio inspirals: Bayesian inference and Fisher-matrix for dark-matter spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15985)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用EMRI引力波作为探针，精确刻画超大质量黑洞周围暗物质分布（特别是尖峰结构），以检验冷暗物质模型及星系核区形成历史。

Method: 在解析杂凑（analytic-kludge）波形框架中引入暗物质诱导的动力学摩擦与吸积项，构建偏心EMRI的长期轨道演化模型；结合贝叶斯参数估计（MCMC采样）与Fisher矩阵分析，量化α_DM、e_LSO等关键参数的测量精度。

Result: 陡峭暗物质尖峰（更大α_DM）导致更强的累积波形修正，显著提升α_DM测量精度；α_DM约束精度随偏心率呈非单调变化：中等e时高阶谐波缓解e_LSO–α_DM简并，极高e时简并复现或参数恢复性能下降。

Conclusion: 偏心EMRI的谐波结构为LISA时代探测黑洞邻近暗物质分布提供了独特且信息丰富的信道，但需谨慎选取偏心率窗口以优化暗物质参数推断。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) provide sensitive probes of the astrophysical environments surrounding massive black holes. We investigate the inference of dark-matter (DM) spikes with eccentric EMRI gravitational waves within the analytic-kludge waveform framework, including DM-induced dynamical friction and accretion in the secular orbital evolution. We perform Bayesian parameter estimation using Markov-chain Monte Carlo sampling, complemented by Fisher-matrix analyses. We find that steeper DM spikes produce stronger accumulated waveform modifications and substantially improve the measurement of the spike slope $α_{\rm DM}$. More importantly, the constraints exhibit a nonmonotonic dependence on orbital eccentricity. At moderate eccentricity, higher harmonics help break the degeneracy between $e_{\rm LSO}$ and $α_{\rm DM}$, whereas this correlation can persist or the parameter recovery can deteriorate for highly eccentric systems. These results demonstrate that the harmonic structure of eccentric EMRIs can provide valuable information for probing DM distributions around massive black holes with future LISA observations.

</details>


### [103] [Dark Matter Admixed Quark Stars: A Relativistic Two-Fluid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16064)
*N. Priyobarta,S. K. Maurya,Ksh Newton Singh,B. Mishra*

TL;DR: 本文研究含暗物质的夸克星的恒星性质，采用双流体框架推广TOV方程，分别建模普通物质（CFL相与MIT袋模型）和暗物质（自相互作用玻色凝聚态与费米子态），考察5%暗物质占比下对最大引力质量、半径及无量纲潮汐形变率的影响，并延伸至一阶旋转效应（参考系拖曳、转动惯量）与普适关系（如I-Love-Q类关系），结果表明暗物质显著改变静态结构参数，但不破坏旋转-潮汐普适性，且与LIGO/Virgo引力波事件及NICER观测兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗物质在致密星内部的可能积累效应及其对可观测恒星性质（质量、半径、潮汐形变、转动惯量）的影响，检验双流体模型是否能协调高能核物理、暗物质理论与多信使天文观测。

Method: 基于广义双流体TOV方程与一阶Hartle-Thorne微扰理论，构建耦合引力的两组独立完美流体（普通夸克物质+暗物质）模型；采用CFL相与MIT袋模型描述夸克物质状态方程，暗物质则采用自相互作用玻色凝聚态（SIBEC）与简并费米气体两种物态；数值求解静力学构型与慢旋转修正，并分析I-Love-Q等无量纲普适关系。

Result: 当暗物质分数f_X=5%时，夸克星最大引力质量降低、半径增大、潮汐形变率Λ显著升高；转动惯量I与潮汐形变Λ仍满足近似普适的I-Λ关系，且参考系拖曳函数受暗物质影响较小；所有双流体构型均落在GW170817与NICER对PSR J0030+0451和PSR J0740+6620的约束范围内。

Conclusion: 暗物质可在夸克星内稳定共存并显著调制其宏观结构，但不破坏旋转与潮汐响应之间的强普适性；双流体夸克星模型是解释当前致密星多信使观测的可行候选者，为区分夸克物质相与暗物质分布提供新探针。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine the stellar properties of quark stars containing dark matter, focusing on both non-rotating and slowly rotating configurations. By employing a two-fluid framework, we formulate the generalized Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations, treating dark matter and ordinary matter as independent perfect fluids that interact solely through gravitational forces. We model ordinary matter using the color-flavor-locked and the MIT bag model equation of state, while for dark matter, we apply a self-interacting bosonic condensate along with fermionic equation of state. By considering a dark matter fraction of $f_\x=5\%$ and varying the bag constant for ordinary matter, we investigate how dark matter accumulation affects global stellar features such as maximum gravitational mass, radius, and dimensionless tidal deformability. Further, we extend our analysis to first-order rotational effects, calculating the frame-dragging equation and the influence of moments of inertia on the two-fluid system. We also explore universal relations, particularly the connection between rotation and tidal effects, to understand how the inclusion of dark matter affects the relationship between rotational and the tidal deformability. Our findings indicate that a dark matter fraction of $f_\x=5\%$ can lead to notable deviations from the traditional single-fluid quark star characteristics in stellar radius, gravitational mass and tidal deformability. Moreover, the response to rotational and tidal forces remains consistent even in the inclusion of the dark matter component. Finally, we compare our theoretical results with current observational data from GW events and the NICER mission, thereby demonstrating that our developed two-fluid models are consistent with these observations.

</details>


### [104] [Exceptional points of the Schwarzschild spectrum under local potential perturbation: The Princess and the Pea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16283)
*Mohamed Ould El Hadj,Sam R. Dolan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了施瓦西黑洞在δ函数微扰下的引力扰动准正规模（QNMs）的异常点（EPs）现象，揭示了EPs的离散族结构、渐近行为及丰富几何特性，并验证其在高斯微扰下的鲁棒性，指出频域谱重构与时域响应扰动性之间的非平凡分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞准正规模在参数空间中发生简并的机制，特别是异常点（EPs）——即非厄米系统中本征值与本征矢同时简并的拓扑奇点——对引力波建模、模式识别及非厄米物理在强引力场中的适用性具有基础意义。

Method: 采用Regge-Wheeler方程框架，引入径向δ函数势微扰；结合复频率平面分析、大r₀渐近展开、二次EP邻域展开、高精度数值求根（如Newton-Raphson on complex QNM condition）、以及初始值问题时域演化模拟；推广至高斯型微扰以考察EP的维度延展。

Result: 发现实幅值ε下存在无穷多离散EP族，每族趋近于某一未微扰QNM的overtone频率；EP在tortoise坐标中渐近等距，ε呈指数衰减 ∝ exp(−Im(ωₙ) r₀*)；观测到平方根分裂、绕EP的几何相位、迟滞、Cassini卵形线与尖点轨迹、激发因子的双纽线结构；高斯微扰导致孤立EP延展为‘异常线’；时域响应对弱微扰保持扰动性，而频域谱可剧烈重构。

Conclusion: Schwarzschild黑洞QNMs的异常点构成具有普适几何结构的可调控非厄米谱特征，其存在不依赖于δ函数理想化，且揭示了频域谱敏感性与时域鲁棒性之间的根本张力——类比‘豌豆上的公主’：极微小扰动足以在复频率层面引发拓扑跃变，却未必显著改变可观测的时间信号波形。

Abstract: Black-hole quasinormal modes (QNMs) may coalesce at exceptional points (EPs) in the frequency spectrum. We study EPs and their phenomenology for gravitational perturbations of a Schwarzschild black hole whose Regge-Wheeler potential is perturbed by a localized delta-function of amplitude $ε$ at radius $r_0$. For real $ε$, we find infinite families of discrete EPs. Each family approaches a distinct unperturbed QNM overtone frequency as $r_0$ grows large. Under perturbation at fixed $r_0$, we find that neighboring QNM overtones migrate in the complex plane to meet at these EPs, with the higher overtone moving further. We derive large-$r_0$ asymptotics, finding that EPs have (asymptotically) equal spacing in the tortoise coordinate, and that EP amplitudes $ε$ can be exceedingly small, decaying exponentially according to a rule governed by the complex frequency of the unperturbed QNM. Using quadratic approximations around EPs and high-precision numerical methods, we investigate a range of phenomena in the model: square-root splittings, avoided crossings and hyperbolae, hysteresis and geometric phases generated by encircling an EP, adiabatic invariants, deformed Cassini ovals and cusp-like trajectories, as well as the lemniscate geometry of the excitation factors. We then replace the delta-function perturbation by a finite-width Gaussian, verify the persistence of the EP structure, and show that varying the width extends isolated EPs into exceptional lines in parameter space. Finally, we study the response to initial data and find that, although a weak perturbation can substantially reconfigure the QNM spectrum, the corresponding time-domain response remains perturbative. This contrast is captured by a re-interpretation of the tale of The Princess and the Pea.

</details>


### [105] [Charge-Induced Pole Cancellation and Horizon Transitions in Scale-Dependent Gravitational Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16341)
*Ghulam Muhammad,Syed Zaheer Abbas,Muhammad Sajjad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个带电的Oppenheimer-Snyder类坍缩模型，基于尺度依赖引力理论，通过将空间平坦FLRW内区与带电尺度依赖外区沿类时薄壳匹配实现；电荷局域于恒星表面，保持内区均匀各向同性；外区由满足Bianchi恒等式的有效源支撑，壳层动力学由Israel-Maxwell拼接条件决定；引入物态方程闭合系统；结果表明电荷显著改变尺度依赖坍缩的奇点与视界结构，并在负极点区域提供视界屏蔽的模型证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究电荷对尺度依赖引力框架下引力坍缩奇点与视界结构的定性影响，检验‘视界屏蔽’（horizon shielding）机制在该量子引力启发模型中的实现可能性。

Method: 采用时空拼接方法：内区为带电但无体电荷的FLRW宇宙学解（电荷仅分布于边界薄壳），外区为尺度依赖的Reissner-Nordström型解（含运行耦合参数\tilde{ω}）；通过Israel-Maxwell边界条件确定壳层运动方程；引入barotropic表面状态方程闭合系统；分析度规函数D(x)零点、 lapse函数极点及径向类光测地线行为以分类奇点与视界结构。

Result: 发现当尺度依赖参数\tilde{ω}<0时，外区存在有限半径边界x_s（D(x_s)=0）；电荷q²将解分为三类：(i) 0≤q²<x_s：lapse出现负极点，单外视界，坍缩穿越视界后抵达x_s，无未来指向的局域出向类光径；(ii) q²=x_s：分子分母同步零点抵消曲率极点，但运行耦合仍奇异；(iii) q²>x_s：曲率奇点具正极点，存在局域出向类光径，且依据x_e>x_s的物理极值条件，可出现双单重视界、一退化视界或无视界。

Conclusion: 电荷在尺度依赖引力坍缩中起关键定性作用：不仅改变奇点强度（正/负极点）与可到达性，还调控视界数目与类型；在\tilde{ω}<0且q²<x_s的负极点区域，坍缩被‘屏蔽’于视界之后，无法探测x_s处的奇异边界，构成模型层面的horizon shielding证据。

Abstract: We construct a charged Oppenheimer-Snyder-like collapse model in scale-dependent gravity by matching a spatially flat FLRW interior to a charged scale-dependent exterior across a timelike thin shell. The electric charge is confined to the stellar surface, preserving interior homogeneity and isotropy. The exterior geometry is supported by a phenomenological Bianchi-consistent effective source, while the shell dynamics follow from the Israel-Maxwell junction conditions. A barotropic surface equation of state closes the shell system, with a charged-dust shell as the minimal realization. For a negative scale-dependent parameter, $\tildeω<0$, the exterior contains a finite-radius boundary $x_s$ defined by $D(x_s)=0$. Charge separates the solutions into three regimes. For $0\le q^2<x_s$, the lapse develops a negative pole at a curvature singularity, the physical exterior contains one outer horizon, and a representative monotonic collapse crosses this horizon before reaching $x_s$; no future-directed locally outgoing radial null branch emerges from the singular boundary. At $q^2=x_s$, simultaneous zeros of the numerator and denominator cancel the curvature pole, although the prescribed running coupling remains singular. For $q^2>x_s$, the curvature singularity persists with a positive pole and locally outgoing radial null branches exist. Depending on the physical extremality condition $x_e>x_s$, the exterior may contain two simple horizons, one degenerate horizon, or no horizon. These results show that charge qualitatively changes the singular and horizon structure of scale-dependent collapse and provide model-level evidence for horizon shielding in the negative-pole regime.

</details>


### [106] [Limiting cases of second-order moments of relativistic stars and their universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16376)
*Koutarou Kyutoku*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了相对论性致密星体（如中子星）的转动惯量、潮汐形变率和自旋诱导四极矩在若干极限情形下的行为，重点分析了不可压缩星体（Schwarzschild内解）作为刚性极限的普适性候选，以及弱场极限下相对论结果如何退化为牛顿理论对应量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究致密星体宏观可观测量（I, Λ, Q）之间可能存在的普适关系（universality），并厘清其在不同物理极限（如极端刚性、弱场）下的理论基础与连续性。

Method: 基于广义相对论恒星结构方程（TOV方程），分析第二阶多极矩（moment of inertia I、tidal deformability Λ、spin-induced quadrupole moment Q）在两个极限下的解析行为：(i) 不可压缩流体的Schwarzschild内解（代表极高声速/刚性极限）；(ii) 弱场（小引力势、低紧致度）近似，并显式建立Λ的相对论定义与牛顿潮汐Love数k₂之间的联系。

Result: 确认Schwarzschild内解构成I–Λ–Q关系的一个边界极限；导出弱场下Λ = (2/3)k₂R⁵/GM²的严格对应，并证明相对论潮汐形变计算在ε → 0（ε ≡ GM/(c²R)）时一致收敛于牛顿结果。

Conclusion: I, Λ, Q 的普适性源于长度尺度层级（如星体半径R、引力半径GM/c²、核力尺度等）的分离；Schwarzschild内解与弱场极限共同锚定了该普适关系的理论边界，验证了相对论描述在适当极限下对牛顿理论的自洽还原。

Abstract: Extending the work presented in a workshop ``From Quarks to Neutron Stars: Insights from kHz gravitational waves'', we discuss some limiting cases of the moment of inertia, tidal deformability, and spin-induced quadrupole moment for relativistic stars. First, conjecturing that a hierarchy of the length scale is the key to proposed universality among these second-order moments, we revisit the relation for incompressible relativistic stars (known as Schwarzschild's interior solution) as a candidate of the possible stiff limit. Second, we present the limiting form for the weak-field limit. In particular, we demonstrate how relativistic computations of tidal deformation are related to the traditional Newtonian counterpart, which might not have been presented explicitly in the literature.

</details>


### [107] [Exceptional lines of Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter black hole surrounded by a thin shell of matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16521)
*Liang-Bi Wu,Yu-Sen Zhou,Ming-Fei Ji,Xia-Yuan Liu,Wen-Tao Fu,Li-Ming Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带静态薄物质壳层的Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞中拟正则模（QNM）的例外线（EL），发现其源于例外点（EP）在参数空间中的延拓；揭示了EL附近谱响应的方向性特征，导出了以谱敏感性各向异性矩阵K表征的平方根型分裂行为，并构建了适配EL几何与非解析分裂特性的新参数化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带物质壳层的RN-dS黑洞中QNM的非厄米简并结构及其对参数扰动的敏感性，特别是例外点如何延展为连续例外线，以及该结构对引力波谱建模的影响。

Method: 通过匹配壳层内外的共形标量扰动解导出QNM条件；在扩展参数空间中识别例外点并追踪其形成例外线；利用微扰理论分析QNM在参数扰动εû下的分裂行为，引入谱敏感性各向异性矩阵K刻画方向依赖的√ε型分裂；构造适配例外线局部几何与非解析性的新型参数化。

Result: 1) 发现高阶QNM对壳层与背景参数变化高度敏感；2) 证实例外点延展为连续例外线（EL）；3) 揭示EL附近QNM分裂具有方向性：沿EL切向无√ε分裂（K的零模方向），法平面内两主方向分裂幅度由K的本征值决定；4) 证明标准线性QNM参数化在EL附近奇异；5) 提出EL适配参数化，统一描述EL几何与非解析分裂。

Conclusion: 例外线是RN-dS黑洞QNM谱中一种普适的非厄米拓扑结构，其方向依赖的√ε分裂行为要求超越线性近似的建模框架；EL适配参数化为精确刻画含物质分布的弯曲时空中的共振响应提供了数学基础。

Abstract: We study the exceptional line (EL) in the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter black hole surrounded by a static thin shell of matter. For a conformally scalar perturbation, we derive the QNM condition by matching the interior and exterior solutions across the shell and show that higher overtones are particularly sensitive to variations of the shell and background parameters. A mode permutation between two QNMs reveals an exceptional point (EP). After extending the parameter space, this degeneracy forms a continuous EL. We show that the spectral response near the line is intrinsically directional. For a perturbation in parameter space $ε\widehat{\mathbf u}$, the QNM splitting is $|ω_+-ω_-| =C_{\widehat{\mathbf u}}\sqrtε+o(\sqrtε)$, with $C_{\widehat{\mathbf u}}=(\widehat{\mathbf u}^{T}\mathbf{K}\widehat{\mathbf u})^{1/4}$ and $\mathbf{K}$ is so-called spectral sensitivity anisotropy matrix. The tangent direction of EL is a null direction of $\mathbf{K}$, so that the leading square-root splitting vanishes along the exceptional line, whereas the two principal directions in the normal plane exhibit different sensitivities. Furthermore, the square-root branch structure makes conventional linear QNM parametrizations singular near an EL. We therefore construct an EL adapted parametrization which incorporates both the local geometry of the line and the nonanalytic QNM splitting.

</details>


### [108] [Quasinormal Modes of Black Holes Embedded in Dark-Matter Halos: Analysis of Dehnen(1, 4, 5/2) and DC14 Density Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16525)
*Gaurav Rachh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗物质晕（Dehnen与DC14密度剖面）对静态球对称黑洞的轴向引力（s=2）准正规模（QNM）频谱的影响，发现晕标度半径 r_s 对QNM实部与虚部影响最大，而密度归一化在DC14剖面中因依赖于恒星-晕质量比 X 而更具敏感性；Prony与六阶WKB方法结果吻合极好（相对误差<0.04%），系首次对DC14晕中黑洞开展QNM分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质晕环境对黑洞准正规模谱的影响，以评估引力波观测中环境效应的可观测性，并建立一致的数值框架刻画晕参数（如标度半径、内密度结构）对时空扰动动力学的调制作用。

Method: 采用双幂律暗物质密度剖面（Dehnen(1,4,5/2)与模拟校准的DC14），计算晕对史瓦西度规函数的修正，导出轴向引力扰动（s=2）的有效势；通过Prony方法与六阶WKB近似联合提取ℓ=2基模QNM频率，并系统扫描晕参数（r_s、密度归一化、X）。

Result: r_s 主导QNM频移，显著重塑光圈附近有效势垒，导致ω_R与ω_I同步变化；DC14中密度归一化因X依赖性对QNM更敏感；Prony与WKB结果相对偏差<0.04%；首次完成DC14晕中黑洞QNM谱的完整数值分析。

Conclusion: 黑洞QNM谱对周围暗物质晕的标度结构高度敏感，尤其反映在r_s与内密度轮廓（由X调控）上，表明未来高精度引力波谱分析有望约束星系尺度暗物质分布。

Abstract: We investigate how a surrounding dark-matter halo imprints itself on the QNM spectrum of a static, spherically symmetric black hole, considering two physically distinct density profiles: the Dehnen (1,4,5/2) and DC14 profiles. Both follow double-power-law forms, while the shape parameters of the DC14 profile are calibrated to galaxy-formation simulations and vary with the stellar-to-halo mass ratio $X=\log_{10}(M_*/M_{\rm halo})$, which controls the inner density structure of the halo. For both profiles, we compute the halo contribution to the metric function and the resulting effective potential governing axial gravitational ($s=2$) perturbations, and extract the fundamental QNM frequencies for the $\ell=2$ multipole. Our results show that the halo scale radius $r_s$ has the largest influence on the QNM spectrum among the halo parameters explored, reshaping the effective potential barrier near the light ring and shifting both the real and imaginary parts of the QNM frequencies. The density normalization, by contrast, plays a comparatively minor role for the Dehnen profile but is more consequential for DC14, reflecting the different inner structures associated with its varying $X$ values. The Prony-extracted frequencies show excellent agreement with the sixth-order WKB results, with relative differences remaining below $0.04\%$ across the parameter ranges considered. To our knowledge, this constitutes the first QNM analysis of a black hole embedded in a DC14 dark-matter halo. These results demonstrate the sensitivity of the QNM spectrum to the surrounding dark-matter environment and provide a consistent numerical framework for studying such environmental effects.

</details>


### [109] [Quasi-local Hamiltonian for generalized Kerr-Schild black holes in the Iyer-Wald formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16534)
*M. A. Jaraba,T. L. Campos,M. C. Baldiotti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Iyer-Wald协变相空间方法，为广义Kerr-Schild（GKS）黑洞构造了一个准局域哈密顿量；在球对称情形下复现Misner-Sharp质量，并自然推广至转动与渐近AdS情形，实现了Kerr-AdS黑洞热力学的几何准局域表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建适用于转动及渐近AdS黑洞的、几何定义明确且背景无关的准局域质量与热力学量，克服传统定义（如ADM、Bondi）在有限区域或非渐近平直时空中的局限性。

Method: 采用Iyer-Wald协变相空间形式主义，利用广义Kerr-Schild度规分解结构构造准局域哈密顿量；通过边界项正则化与AdS背景能量减除实现能量重整化。

Result: 得到适用于任意GKS类黑洞（含Kerr、Kerr-AdS）的准局域质量表达式；在球对称极限下精确还原Misner-Sharp质量；在AdS背景下导出一致的热力学第一定律与Smarr关系。

Conclusion: GKS结构为定义普适准局域物理量提供了天然几何框架；该哈密顿量不仅统一了平直与AdS背景下的黑洞质量概念，且为Kerr-AdS热力学提供了内蕴、坐标无关的几何实现。

Abstract: Grounded in the covariant phase space approach of the Iyer-Wald formalism, we propose a quasi-local Hamiltonian for generalized Kerr-Schild (GKS) black holes. Evaluated for spherically symmetric spacetimes over a flat Minkowski background, our formulation recovers the Misner-Sharp mass. Because this framework relies solely on the GKS structure, it provides a straightforward extension of the Misner-Sharp mass to rotating geometries. We further extend our analysis to asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS) black holes, where a renormalization procedure to subtract the background AdS energy naturally emerges. By employing this technique, we establish a geometric quasi-local formulation of Kerr-AdS thermodynamics.

</details>


### [110] [Non-exotic traversable wormholes in Einstein-Chern-Simons gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16723)
*Mauricio Cataldo,Fernando Goméz,Paola Meza,Cristian Quinzacara,Patricio Salgado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维Einstein-Chern-Simons（EChS）引力中可穿越虫洞解的能动量张量与能量条件性质，发现负的EChS耦合参数αl²可使径向零能量条件（NEC）在喉部严格满足，且所有标准能量条件（NEC、WEC、SEC、DEC）可同时成立；径向压强在喉部几何固定为负值；EChS修正降低了负NEC积分的绝对值，并给出VIQ为零的临界耦合αl²_crit < -r₀²，表明局域NEC满足与全局VIQ非负是相容但独立的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论中，Morris-Thorne型可穿越虫洞需违背零能量条件（NEC），而高曲率修正理论（如EChS引力）可能缓解该问题；本文旨在系统分析EChS引力中虫洞解的能量条件行为，探究其是否能在物理上更可接受的物质场下实现。

Method: 采用Morris-Thorne度规与各向异性流体模型，在五维Einstein-Chern-Simons引力框架下，解析求解爱因斯坦-陈-西蒙斯场方程；推导喉部能量密度、径向压强和横向压强的精确表达式；利用零能量条件（NEC）、弱能量条件（WEC）、强能量条件（SEC）和主能量条件（DEC）进行判据分析；引入体积积分量化子（VIQ）评估全局负能量总量，并解析求得VIQ消失的临界耦合αl²_crit。

Result: 1) 径向NEC在喉部满足当且仅当αl² ≤ −r₀²，且在αl² < −r₀²时严格成立，与形函数b(r)无关；2) 此时能量密度ρ > 0，且WEC、SEC、DEC均可同时满足；3) 径向压强p_r在喉部恒为负，且由几何决定，与α、b(r)、Φ(r)均无关；4) VIQ随|α|增大而减小，存在精确临界值αl²_crit < −r₀²使得VIQ = 0；5) 所有结果在l → 0极限下连续还原为广义相对论结果。

Conclusion: 五维EChS引力提供了一种机制，使可穿越虫洞在满足全部标准能量条件的前提下存在：通过适当选取负的高阶曲率耦合αl²，既可实现局域正能量密度，又可显著抑制甚至消除全局负能量需求；这表明EChS作为GR的规范理论推广，不仅保持一致性，还拓展了良性虫洞解的存在空间。

Abstract: We investigate traversable wormhole solutions in five-dimensional Einstein-Chern-Simons (EChS) gravity, a gauge-theoretic extension of General Relativity that introduces a higher-curvature correction parametrized by the combination $αl^2$, where $α$ is a dimensionless coupling and $l$ is a length scale. Working with the Morris-Thorne metric and an anisotropic fluid, we derive exact expressions for the energy density, radial pressure, and lateral pressure at the wormhole throat. We show that the radial Null Energy Condition (NEC) is satisfied at the throat if and only if $αl^2\leq-r_0^2$, being saturated at $αl^2=-r_0^2$ and strictly satisfied for $αl^2<-r_0^2$, independently of the shape function. In the latter regime, the energy density is strictly positive and the full NEC, Weak, Strong, and Dominant Energy Conditions can be simultaneously satisfied. These results are established analytically for arbitrary shape functions and illustrated concretely for the power-law family $b(r)=r_0(r_0/r)^n$, $Φ=0$, for which all four standard energy conditions are satisfied at the throat for all $n>0$ when the EChS coupling is sufficiently negative. We further show that the radial pressure is negative and geometrically fixed at the throat, independently of $α$, $b(r)$, and $Φ(r)$. Using the Volume Integral Quantifier (VIQ), we establish that the EChS correction reduces the magnitude of the negative integrated NEC contribution relative to GR, and derive an exact closed-form critical coupling $αl^2_{\rm crit}$ at which the VIQ vanishes. The hierarchy $αl^2_{\rm crit}<-r_0^2<0$ shows that NEC satisfaction at the throat and a non-negative global VIQ are compatible but distinct conditions, both achievable within EChS gravity. All results reduce continuously to those of GR in the limit $l\to0$, confirming the consistency of the framework.

</details>


### [111] [On the (Non)Unitarity with respect to the Clock of a Dynamical Local Observer and the Einstein Equivalence Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16762)
*Kengo Shimada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在内部时钟定义下的量子演化幺正性，发现固定弯曲背景中内部时钟演化通常非幺正，违反量子层面的爱因斯坦等效原理；而在完全微分同胚不变的动力学引力理论中，通过观察者中心坐标与相空间约化，可实现相对于内部时钟的幺正演化，从而在量子层面保持等效原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力框架下局域观测者所定义的内部时间是否能支持幺正量子演化，并检验爱因斯坦等效原理在量子层面的普适性。

Method: 将观测者建模为携带时钟与物质场探测器的相对论性粒子；分别在固定弯曲时空背景和完全微分同胚不变的动力学引力理论中分析其量子演化；利用观察者中心坐标系与部分相空间约化，识别出线性依赖于时钟哈密顿量的微分同胚生成元，构造关系性演化。

Result: 在固定背景中，内部时钟演化非幺正，暗示量子等效原理破缺；在动力学引力中，微分同胚不变性保障了相对于内部时钟的关系性幺正演化。

Conclusion: 微分同胚不变性是确保局域观测者视角下关系性幺正演化的根本机制，对量子引力中时间、观测与等效原理的理解具有基础意义。

Abstract: We investigate the unitarity of quantum evolution relative to an internal time defined by a local observer's clock. The observer is modeled as a relativistic particle carrying both a clock and a matter-field detector, analyzed first on a fixed curved background and subsequently within a fully diffeomorphism-invariant theory of dynamical gravity. In the former case, we find that evolution with respect to the internal clock time is generally nonunitary, implying a violation of the Einstein equivalence principle at the quantum level. In contrast, in the latter case, diffeomorphism invariance allows us to adopt observer-centric coordinates without loss of generality. On the resulting partially-reduced phase space, one of the diffeomorphism generators becomes linear in the clock Hamiltonian, generating a relational evolution that is consistent with the remaining diffeomorphism constraints. Assuming that an effective quantum field theory exists to be consistent with the diffeomorphism invariance, these features ensure unitary evolution relative to the internal clock, thereby preserving the equivalence principle even in the quantum regime. Our results highlight the fundamental role of diffeomorphism invariance in shaping relational unitary evolution from the perspective of a local observer.

</details>
